			else = {
				from = {

				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}

				}
			}

			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = dtp.30032
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		from = {
			dxxohrp = yes
			dxgtkrc = yes
			dxdo31j = yes
			set_variable = { which = dtp_rr value = -1 }
		}
		dtp_load = yes
		dtp_sp_load = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_t
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tc
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tc
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_r
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_rcp
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_rcp
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_p
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tp
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tp
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_f
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tf
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tf
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_dis value = 1 }
				}
			}
			dtp_disarm = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 3201 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						from = {
							dxcyym2 = {
								P = species
							}
						}
					}
					event_target:dtp_sp = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_trait = trait_cyborg_creed_of_war
							}
							change_species_characteristics = {
								remove_trait = trait_cyborg_creed_of_war
							}
						}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_log8_actor }
				}
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = 0 }

					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt

			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
